We evaluate 10 AI systems and find that the strongest systems can acquire and apply unfamiliar rules, while performance varies substantially across trajectories and continued exploration can stall or reverse earlier gains. ExplorationBench represents a step towards AI systems that can acquire and apply genuinely new knowledge through exploration in unknown environments.

</details>


### [99] [A Living Benchmark for Information Retrieval from Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30205)
*Jordan L. Cahoon,Chloe O. Stanwyck,Sulaiman Somani,Philip Chung,Kevin R Keet,Kameron C. Black,Andrea T. Fisher,Sarita Khemani,Jerry Liu,Stephen Ma,Saloni K. Maharaj,Rita M. Pandya,Eduardo Perez-Guerrero,Priyanka Pillai,Lisa Shieh,David J. H. Wu,James Xie,James C. McAvoy,Teresa Nguyen,Jessica Tran,Lucy Yin,Bridget Lin,Alison Callahan,Jason A. Fries,Nigam H. Shah,Emily Alsentzer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个可扩展的框架，能够从纵向电子健康记录（EHR）笔记中自动生成问答对，并据此构建了可持续维护的临床LLM评测基准BRIE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的临床助手正被集成进EHR系统，其安全性和实用性依赖严谨评测；但现有基准多靠人工构建、成本高、更新困难，且随技术发展迅速过时。

Method: 提出自动从纵向EHR笔记生成问答对的可扩展框架；由19名临床医生验证生成器，产出BRIE基准；并在9个LLM和5种推理策略上进行评测。

Result: 最先进系统常遗漏临床重要信息，尤其在需要跨多份文档和多次就诊进行综合的问题上；由于生成器经临床验证，BRIE还支持生成反映临床医生推理差异的多个答案，并可持续刷新内容以防数据泄漏。

Conclusion: 可扩展的基准生成方法能够实现对临床LLM的严谨、最新评测，适应快速演变的医疗场景部署需求。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based clinical assistants are increasingly being integrated into electronic health record (EHR) systems, transforming how clinicians retrieve and synthesize information from patient records. Their safety and utility depend on rigorous evaluation, yet existing benchmarks are manually curated, costly to update, and rapidly become obsolete with evolving technological advancements. We present a scalable framework that automatically generates question--answer pairs from longitudinal EHR notes. Nineteen clinicians validate the benchmark generator, producing the Benchmark for Retrieving Information in EHRs (BRIE), a continuously maintainable evaluation dataset. Across nine LLMs and five inference strategies, state-of-the-art systems frequently omit clinically important information, particularly for questions requiring synthesis across multiple documents and encounters. Because the generator itself is validated, BRIE supports evaluations that static benchmarks cannot, including the generation of multiple answers that reflect variation in clinician reasoning for robust performance assessment and continuously refreshing benchmark content to guard against leakage. Our results demonstrate that scalable benchmark generation enables rigorous, up-to-date evaluation of clinical LLMs as they are deployed in rapidly evolving healthcare settings.

</details>


### [100] [Forecast-Dojo: Replayable Environments for Benchmarking and Training LLM Forecasting Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28876)
*Liqin Ye,Haorui Wang,Fardin Ahmed,Rongzhi Zhang,Yuan He,Ziyuan Lin,Yanbin Yin,Jing Peng,Michael Galarnyk,Sudheer Chava,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了 Forecast-Dojo，一个可重放的 LLM 预测智能体基准与训练环境，融合已结算预测市场问题与带日期新闻，支持重复评估与训练；实验显示研究工具普遍提升预测，但所有模型仍落后于历史市场预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测类任务通常需等待事件结算才能获得反馈，评估与训练成本高、周期长。作者希望构建一个可重放环境，使同一批任务既能反复评估，又能收集训练交互，并利用已记录的结果直接提供反馈，无需等待新事件发生。

Method: 构建 Forecast-Dojo 环境，整合已结算的 Polymarket 事件与带日期新闻，让智能体在连续的历史日期上研究事件并反复更新预测。数据含 1,568 个事件（按时间划分为训练期与评估期）和 1,880 万篇带日期新闻。评测 12 个模型，比较研究工具、信念笔记本等设置下的表现，并以监督微调作为训练概念验证。

Result: 在 12 个模型的评估中，研究工具使全部 12 个模型的 Brier 分数下降；随着事件推进，预测持续改善，且在新增带日期证据更多的步骤上提升最大。然而所有模型在 Brier 分数和准确率上仍落后于历史市场预测。跨日期传递的信念笔记本降低了研究成本，但未稳定提升预测质量。

Conclusion: Forecast-Dojo 不仅可用于评估，还能提供交互轨迹与结果反馈以支持智能体学习，监督微调验证了该训练路径的可行性。

Abstract: We introduce Forecast-Dojo, a replayable environment for benchmarking and training LLM forecasting agents. It combines resolved prediction-market questions with dated news, allowing agents to research an event and revisit their predictions at successive historical dates. The same tasks and tools support repeated evaluation, collection of training interactions, and feedback from recorded outcomes without waiting for new events to resolve. Forecast-Dojo contains 1,568 Polymarket events, split by time into training and evaluation periods, and 18.8M dated news articles. In an evaluation of 12 models, research tools lower Brier score for all 12. Forecasts also improve as events unfold, with the largest gains at steps where more newly dated evidence is recorded. Every model still trails historical market forecasts in both Brier score and accuracy. A belief notebook carried between dates lowers research cost but does not consistently improve forecast quality. Beyond evaluation, Forecast-Dojo provides interaction trajectories and outcome feedback for agent learning, with supervised fine-tuning as a proof of concept.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [Thinking Leakage: A Causal Audit of NoThink Post-Training in Hybrid Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28682)
*Zehao Liu,Vasant G. Honavar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出"思维泄漏"概念：后训练混合推理模型在NoThink模式下的性能提升，很大程度上是重新调用了基座模型Think模式中已存在的推理行为，而非真正提升了NoThink能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练混合推理模型在NoThink模式下能同时提升性能并保持快速推理，受到广泛关注。但这些收益可能并非源于能力提升，而是来自基座模型Think模式中本已可获得的思维行为。作者希望厘清这种"思维泄漏"是否真实存在、是否具有因果性、其贡献有多大。

Method: 作者将思维泄漏形式化为因果中介框架，沿一条由基座模型推导出的简单激活方向，采用双向干预（steering与counter-steering）进行审计。实验覆盖3个模型、3种后训练方法，在竞赛数学基准上展开，并结合行为与表征分析。

Result: 结果表明泄漏真实、因果且显著：行为与表征分析显示模型向Think模式偏移；沿该方向steering基座模型可复现后训练带来的大部分准确率增益；对checkpoint进行counter-steering则能消除其获得增益的相当大一部分。在9个具有正向NoThink增益的对齐checkpoint上，泄漏比率为42%至79%。

Conclusion: 思维泄漏对后训练收益有显著的因果贡献。后训练方法表现出的优势可能只是更大程度地向Think模式漂移，从而掩盖了它究竟是真正提升了NoThink内部能力，还是更有效地重新调用已有的Think行为。

Abstract: Post-training hybrid reasoning models in NoThink mode has attracted growing interest as a way to improve performance while keeping inference fast. However, these gains may draw on thinking behavior already accessible through the base model's Think mode. We formulate this thinking leakage in a causal mediation framework and audit its contribution using bidirectional interventions along a simple base-derived activation direction. Across three models and three post-training methods on competition math benchmarks, we find that leakage is real, causal, and substantial: behavioral and representational analyses reveal shifts toward Think, steering the base model along this direction reproduces most of the post-training accuracy gain, and counter-steering a checkpoint removes a substantial share of what it gains. Across nine aligned checkpoints with positive NoThink gains, the resulting leakage ratio ranges from 42% to 79%. These interventions support a substantial causal contribution of thinking leakage. Our findings show that a post-training method's apparent advantage can therefore reflect greater drift toward Think, obscuring whether it improves capability within NoThink or more effectively re-invokes existing Think behavior.

</details>


### [102] [CataOPD: Catalytic On-Policy Distillation for Large Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29518)
*Wenjin Liu,Chenxi Wang,Jiapu Wang,Zhe Cui,Anh Tuan Luu,Haoran Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CataOPD框架，将教师作为“催化剂”而非目标，通过自救援路由、催化引导自解决和障碍加权内化三阶段，把已验证的学生自产轨迹内化为无催化策略，提升大模型推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在无正确轨迹采样时缺乏正向正确信号，而在线策略蒸馏又受限于学生在线分布下可达的推理轨迹，两者在难问题上均受限。

Method: CataOPD包含三部分：Self-Rescue Routing以全失败组作为路由信号，先通过额外在线自采样寻找正确轨迹；对自救援未解决的问题，Catalytic-Guided Self-Resolution利用催化引导在引导学生分布中引出已验证的学生自产轨迹；Barrier-Weighted Internalization按引导与无引导对数概率差对token加权，聚焦难以无引导完成的关键token更新。

Result: 实验表明CataOPD优于现有基线，能将独立学生推理扩展到仍未恢复的问题上，并在无催化推理下提升分布外泛化能力。

Conclusion: CataOPD通过催化剂式教师引导与无催化内化，有效突破RL正向信号缺失与OPD可达性受限的瓶颈，提升推理能力与泛化性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) and on-policy distillation (OPD) are two representative paradigms for improving large language model reasoning. However, when no correct trajectory is sampled, RL lacks a positive correctness signal, while OPD remains constrained by the reasoning trajectories reachable under the student's on-policy distribution. Therefore, we propose CataOPD, where the teacher acts as a catalyst rather than a target, expanding reachability while internalizing verified student-produced trajectories into a catalyst-free policy. Self-Rescue Routing uses empirically all-failed groups as routing signals rather than teacher-intervention triggers, first seeking correct trajectories through additional on-policy self-sampling. For problems unresolved after self-rescue, Catalytic-Guided Self-Resolution uses catalytic guidance to elicit a verified student-produced trajectory in the guided student distribution. Barrier-Weighted Internalization weights tokens by guided-to-unguided log-probability gaps, focusing updates on decisive tokens difficult without guidance. Experimental results show that CataOPD outperforms current baselines, extends independent student reasoning to still-unrecovered problems, and improves out-of-distribution generalization under catalyst-free inference. Our project is available at https://github.com/QwenQKing/CataOPD.

</details>


### [103] [Graph, Loop, and Harness Engineering for Zero-Trust Agentic Data Engineering and Analytical Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29668)
*Sagar Srinivas Sakhinana,Venkataramana Runkana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两个零信任框架：Zero-Trust Agentic Data Engineering 与 Zero-Trust Agentic OLAP，通过证据门控的图工程、有界恢复的循环工程和零信任执行的智能体框架工程三大抽象，实现云数据工程与分析执行的可靠协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型智能体日益自动化数据工作流，但端到端的云数据工程与分析执行需要在代码、数据、基础设施和运行时环境之间实现可靠协调，现有方法在可信验证与授权保障方面存在不足。

Method: 提出两个零信任框架：1）Zero-Trust Agentic Data Engineering：从自然语言任务出发生成、部署并验证完整的云数据工程解决方案，完成条件依赖于仓库、部署、运行时与策略证据；2）Zero-Trust Agentic OLAP：结合受治理的数据准备与经过验证的 OLAP，仅在验证与证据绑定的审批后才能推进生产，且在满足同快照执行、精确结果等价、确定性接地与反思后才发布分析答案。两者共享三大抽象：图工程（证据门控的工作流结构）、循环工程（有界恢复）、智能体框架工程（零信任执行）。

Result: 在名义执行、受控故障、有界恢复和策略约束条件下对两个框架进行评估，衡量指标包括验证完成度、恢复能力、授权执行、生产推进与经过验证的 OLAP 执行。

Conclusion: 两个零信任框架通过共享的图工程、循环工程与智能体框架工程抽象，为云数据工程与分析执行提供了证据驱动、可验证且授权受控的协调方案，并在多种执行与故障条件下完成了系统评估。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly automate data workflows, but end-to-end cloud data engineering and analytical execution require reliable coordination across code, data, infrastructure, and runtime environments. We present two zero-trust frameworks. Zero-Trust Agentic Data Engineering generates, deploys, and verifies complete cloud data-engineering solutions from natural-language tasks, with completion conditioned on repository, deployment, runtime, and policy evidence. Zero-Trust Agentic OLAP combines governed Data Preparation with verified Online Analytical Processing (OLAP), permitting production promotion only after validation and evidence-bound approval, and releasing analytical answers only after Same-Snapshot Execution, Exact Result Equivalence, deterministic grounding, and reflection. Both frameworks share three abstractions: graph engineering for evidence-gated workflow structure, loop engineering for bounded recovery, and agent-harness engineering for zero-trust execution. We evaluate both frameworks under nominal execution, controlled failures, bounded recovery, and policy-constrained conditions, measuring verified completion, recovery, authorization enforcement, production promotion, and verified OLAP execution.

</details>


### [104] [Beyond Average Safety: Chance-Constrained LLM Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29960)
*Taha Entesari,Mahyar Fazlyab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种机会约束微调方法，限制安全样例中退化超阈值的比例，通过可微凸上界与约束感知梯度下降实现尾部感知的安全校正，在三任务三模型上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全保持微调方法多控制平均安全损失或使用加权辅助惩罚，会掩盖罕见但严重的失败（尾部风险），因此需要把安全保持建模为可靠性约束优化问题。

Method: 提出机会约束式安全保持微调：限制相对参考模型退化超过给定阈值的样例占比；由于经验机会约束含不连续指示函数，引入违规率的可微凸上界（majorization），得到可处理的保守约束；进而设计约束感知梯度下降，将近似约束视为参数空间中的安全集，以闭式解最小修改微调方向以保持可行性。

Result: 在三个任务、三个模型上开展有害微调实验，方法一致优于文献中的基线。

Conclusion: LLM微调中的安全保持更适合视为可靠性约束优化，而非平均风险正则化。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models on new objectives can improve helpfulness, instruction following, or domain-specific performance, but it can also induce regressions on safety-critical prompts. Existing safety-preserving fine-tuning methods typically control average safety loss or use weighted auxiliary penalties, which can obscure rare but severe failures. We propose a chance-constrained formulation for safety-preserving fine-tuning that limits the fraction of safety examples whose degradation relative to a reference model exceeds a prescribed threshold. Because the resulting empirical chance constraint contains a discontinuous indicator, we introduce a differentiable majorization of the violation rate, yielding a tractable conservative constraint. We then develop a constraint-aware gradient descent method that treats the majorized constraint as a safe set in parameter space and minimally modifies the fine-tuning direction to preserve feasibility. The resulting update admits a closed form and produces a tail-aware safety correction that emphasizes examples near or above the degradation threshold. We conduct an extensive set of experiments on harmful fine-tuning across three different tasks and three models and show that our approach consistently outperforms the baselines that exist in the literature. These results suggest that safety preservation in LLM fine-tuning is better viewed as a reliability-constrained optimization problem than as average-risk regularization.

</details>


### [105] [Let Training Guide Selection: Online Synthetic Data Filtering via Real-Anchored Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29988)
*Yanran Wu,Sana Lakdawala,Renzo Tassara Miller,Chongyang Bai,Sharath Ciddu,Shivendra Pratap Singh,Kungang Li,Sandeep Pandey,Chunwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 FROST，一个在线合成数据筛选框架，利用锚定真实数据的梯度反馈估计合成样本效用，动态决定何时筛选与保留哪些样本，在图像分类、文本到 SQL 微调及工业广告重排系统中均取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据可缓解真实数据不足时的监督稀缺问题，但噪声与分布不匹配会削弱其价值。已有筛选方法多强调保真度或多样性，未考虑学习器不断变化的需求，且往往依赖外部验证器或留出验证集，成本高、适应性差。

Method: FROST 是一个在线框架：通过锚定真实训练数据的梯度反馈估计合成数据效用；对批次效用与近期历史进行校准，以判断何时需要过滤；仅在"超范围"批次中筛选样本，以决定保留什么，无需外部验证器或留出验证集。

Result: 在两个公开基准（图像分类、面向文本到 SQL 的大模型微调）上，FROST 过滤掉约 20–30% 的合成数据，同时相比使用全部合成数据训练提升了真实任务性能。进一步在大规模工业广告重排系统的训练中应用 FROST，相较高度优化的生产基线取得显著性能提升。

Conclusion: FROST 以学习器需求为中心的在线筛选思路有效且具泛化性，能在过滤部分合成数据的同时提升真实任务表现，并成功落地于工业级应用。

Abstract: Synthetic data can scale training supervision when real-world data are limited, but noise and distribution mismatch can reduce its value. Existing synthetic data selection methods often emphasize fidelity or diversity rather than the learner's evolving needs. We propose FROST, an online framework that estimates synthetic-data utility through gradient feedback anchored in real training data. It calibrates batch utility against recent history to determine when filtering is needed and filters samples only in out-of-band batches to determine what to retain, without an external verifier or held-out validation set. Experiments on two public benchmarks for image classification and LLM fine-tuning for text-to-SQL show that FROST filters out around 20--30% of the synthetic data while improving real-task performance compared with training on the full synthetic data pool. We further apply FROST during training in a large-scale industrial ads re-ranking system, achieving significant performance gains over a highly optimized production baseline, demonstrating its effectiveness and generalizability.

</details>


### [106] [Reachability-Based Formal Verification of Graph Neural Networks with Node and Edge Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30079)
*Anne M. Tumlin,Ben Wooding,Zhenxuan Shao,Diego Manzanas Lopez,Tyler Derr,Taylor T. Johnson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GraphStar集合扩展了神经网络验证（NNV）框架，使其支持图结构输入，从而可对GNN进行形式化验证，并在电力系统任务中提供了更紧密的鲁棒性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN在电力系统（潮流分析、最优潮流估计、连锁故障分析等）中被广泛用作快速拓扑感知代理模型，但对其形式化验证仍具挑战性，现有方法适用范围有限。

Method: 提出GraphStar集合，将Star集合推广到图结构输入，可刻画节点与边特征上的不确定性；该扩展支持线性消息传递操作的传播以及对ReLU非线性的可靠近似，适用于GCN和带边特征的GINE等GNN架构。

Result: 在IEEE-24、IEEE-39、IEEE-118三个电力系统测试用例上的PF、OPF、CFA三项任务以及ENZYMES、PROTEINS两个图分类基准上评测GNNV；结果显示GNNV在基于ReLU激活的图分类模型上比CORA提供更紧密的鲁棒性保证，并首次为GINE-based PF和OPF模型在节点与边联合扰动下提供边感知鲁棒性保证。

Conclusion: GNNV通过GraphStar集合实现了对图结构输入的通用验证扩展，在电力系统与图分类任务中验证有效，并将边感知验证能力拓展到GINE模型。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) have become a prominent approach for developing fast, topology-aware surrogates in electric power systems, supporting tasks such as power flow (PF) analysis, optimal power flow (OPF) estimation, and cascading failure analysis (CFA). Despite this growing use, formally verifying GNN-based models remains challenging, with existing methods limited in scope. We extend the neural network verification (NNV) framework to graph-structured inputs through GraphStar sets, a generalization of Star sets that captures uncertainty over both node and edge features. This extension enables the propagation of linear message-passing operations and the sound approximation of ReLU nonlinearities for GNN architectures, including graph convolutional network (GCN) and graph isomorphism network with edge features (GINE) layers. We evaluate GNNV across three power system tasks, PF, OPF, and CFA, on the IEEE-24, IEEE-39, and IEEE-118 test cases, as well as two standard graph classification benchmarks, ENZYMES and PROTEINS. Our results show that GNNV provides tighter robustness guarantees than CORA on graph classification models with ReLU-based activations and, for the first time, delivers edge-aware robustness guarantees for GINE-based PF and OPF models under joint node and edge perturbations.

</details>


### [107] [Residual Correlation as a Diagnostic for Joint-Uncertainty Gains from GP Coregionalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30085)
*Fangqin Zhou,Joaquin Vanschoren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现多输出GP-ICM协同建模的主要收益在于联合不确定性量化而非点预测，并提出诊断指标D_logdet来预测耦合何时有效，同时提出Residual-ICM方法取得最优平均联合NLL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标回归中常假设通过共享统计强度（GP-ICM）能提升整体性能，但实际收益并不一致，因此需要理解协同建模何时有效以及为何有效。

Method: 1) 系统分析核心区域化的收益来源；2) 提出轻量级诊断指标 D_logdet = -1/2 log det R_res，可从独立GP计算，代表理想化联合NLL增益；3) 提出Residual-ICM，保留独立边际方差同时向联合协方差添加残差相关结构。

Result: 点预测几乎不变（ΔR²≈0）；D_logdet能强烈预测ICM的NLL改善（ρ_s=-0.83, p<0.001），优于特征样本比等启发式；Residual-ICM在比较方法中取得最佳平均联合NLL。实验涵盖合成研究、16个多目标基准及冻结的transformer和CNN关键点回归表示。

Conclusion: 核心区域化的主要收益是联合不确定性量化；残差相关（而非原始目标相关）是联合不确定性增益的最强预测因子；该诊断适用于全局高斯残差依赖（即可分离核心区域化所捕获的结构）。

Abstract: In multi-target regression, correlated targets are often coupled through multi-output Gaussian processes with an intrinsic model of coregionalisation (GP-ICM), assuming that sharing statistical strength improves overall performance. In practice, the benefits are inconsistent. Across the settings studied, we find that the main benefit of coregionalisation is joint uncertainty quantification rather than point prediction. Raw target correlation does not predict when coupling helps; in the separable GP-ICM settings studied here, residual correlation, the cross-target dependence left unexplained by independent per-target predictors, is the strongest predictor of joint-uncertainty gains.
  We introduce a lightweight diagnostic, $D_{\rm logdet}=-\frac{1}{2}\log\det R_{\rm res}$, which represents the idealised joint negative log-likelihood (NLL) gain from modelling a full rather than diagonal residual covariance and is computable from independent GPs alone. Across a controlled synthetic study, 16 multi-target benchmarks, and frozen transformer and convolutional neural network representations for keypoint regression, point prediction remains largely unchanged ($ΔR^2\approx 0$). In contrast, $D_{\rm logdet}$ strongly predicts observed ICM NLL improvements ($ρ_s=-0.83$, $p<0.001$), outperforming heuristics such as the feature-to-sample ratio. We also propose Residual-ICM, which preserves independent marginal variances while adding residual-correlation structure to the joint covariance. Residual-ICM achieves the best average joint NLL among the compared methods, while the diagnostic indicates when covariance coupling is likely to be useful. The diagnostic is specific to global Gaussian residual dependence, the structure captured by separable coregionalisation.

</details>


### [108] [AT-SKM-Net: An Accelerated Trainable Sampling Kaczmarz-Motzkin Framework for Linear Hard-Constraint Feasibility on Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30088)
*Xiaochen Zhang,Haoyu Zhu,Yao Zhang,Qingchun Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AT-SKM Net框架，通过拓扑感知异构图神经网络引导混合采样并引入Cholesky更新机制，将等式投影复杂度从O(N^3)降至O(N^2)，在保证零约束违反的同时大幅减少迭代次数并加速SKM层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向关键基础设施的图结构线性约束优化面临海量严格硬约束与高维度带来的可扩展性瓶颈。现有基于投影的方法（如T-SKM-Net）虽能保证可行性，但在动态环境中需处理全部约束集并进行昂贵的矩阵分解，计算成本高昂。

Method: 提出Accelerated Trainable-SKM（AT-SKM）Net框架：1）设计由拓扑感知异构GNN模型引导的混合采样策略，将计算集中于活跃约束，消除冗余计算；2）针对图约束的拓扑变化，采用Cholesky更新机制，在低秩扰动下将等式投影复杂度理论上从O(N^3)降低到O(N^2)。

Result: 在随机几何图、N-1安全约束DC-OPF以及最小成本天然气输送问题上进行实验，AT-SKM将迭代次数最多减少85%，SKM层实现2.95倍至7.29倍加速，同时保持零约束违反。

Conclusion: AT-SKM Net通过GNN引导的活跃约束采样与Cholesky更新，有效缓解了投影类方法在动态图约束环境中的计算瓶颈，在保证可行性的前提下显著提升了求解效率，适用于大规模图结构约束优化任务。

Abstract: Graph-structured optimization with linear constraints is fundamental to critical infrastructure but faces scalability limits due to massive strict hard constraints and high dimensionality. While recent projection-based methods such as Trainable Sampling Kaczmarz-Motzkin Net (T-SKM-Net) guarantee feasibility, they face high computational costs in dynamic environments by processing the entire constraint set and requiring expensive matrix factorizations. To bridge this gap, we propose the Accelerated Trainable-SKM (AT-SKM) Net framework. To concentrate computation on the active constraints and eliminate redundant calculations, we introduce a hybrid sampling strategy guided by a topology-aware heterogeneous GNN model. To efficiently handle topological shifts in graph-based constraints, we employ a Cholesky Update mechanism that theoretically reduces the equality projection complexity from O(N^3) to O(N^2) under low-rank perturbations. Experiments on random geometric graphs, N-1 Security-Constrained DC-OPF, and minimum-cost gas transport problem demonstrate that AT-SKM reduces iteration counts by up to 85% and achieves 2.95x-7.29x SKM layer speedups, while maintaining zero constraint violations.

</details>


### [109] [On the SoS Certifiability of Log-Concave Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30105)
*Aleksandr Storozhenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了任意各向同性对数凹分布在任意偶数阶 m 下的中心矩可用平方和 (SoS) 多项式证书控制（常数与 Poincaré 常数无关），从而恢复对数凹分布的最优矩界并获得无维度误差的高维统计估计算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kothari 与 Steinhardt 的结果依赖 Poincaré 常数，无法给出对数凹分布的最优矩界。作者希望去除这一依赖，使 SoS 证书在无额外假设下成立，并由此得到具有无维度误差保证的高效统计算法。

Method: 利用随机局部化 (stochastic localization) 将分布 P 分解为随机强对数凹测度的平均，这些测度的中心矩具有 Diakonikolas-Hopkins-Pensia-Tiegel 的次高斯证书。通过协方差自适应的局部化选择，证明由 Letwin 关于二次型的方差不等式导出的四阶矩证书足以在任意偶数阶控制该平均过程。

Result: 对任意各向同性对数凹分布 P 与任意偶数 m≥2，多项式 (Cm)^m‖v‖₂^m − E⟨X,v⟩^m 是平方和，其中 C 为通用常数。作为直接推论，可为广泛的高维统计估计问题给出计算高效且误差保证与维度无关的算法。

Conclusion: 该工作移除了 Kothari-Steinhardt 定理中的 Poincaré 常数依赖，恢复了对数凹分布的最优矩界，并建立了随机局部化与 SoS 证书之间的技术桥梁，为高维统计估计提供无维度误差的算法保证。

Abstract: For an arbitrary isotropic log-concave distribution $P$ on $\mathbb{R}^d$, we prove that the polynomial $(Cm)^m\|v\|_2^m - \mathbb{E}_{X\sim P}\langle X,v\rangle^m$ is a sum of squares for every even $m\ge2$, where $C>0$ is a universal constant. This removes the dependence on the Poincaré constant in the theorem of Kothari and Steinhardt (arXiv:1711.07465), recovering the optimal moment bounds for log-concave distributions. As an immediate corollary, we obtain computationally efficient algorithms with dimension-free error guarantees for a wide range of high-dimensional statistical estimation problems.
  Our proof uses stochastic localization to decompose $P$ as an average of random strongly log-concave measures, whose centered moments admit the subgaussian certificates of Diakonikolas, Hopkins, Pensia, and Tiegel (STOC 2025; arXiv:2410.21194). With a covariance-adapted choice of localization, we show that a fourth-moment certificate derived from Letwin's variance inequality for quadratic forms (arXiv:2607.24164) suffices to control this averaging at every even degree.

</details>


### [110] [Beyond Compression: Training Latent Representations for Stable Long-Horizon Rollout in Neural Surrogate Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30198)
*Andreas E. Robertson,Ashley T. Lenau,John D. Shimanek,Benjamin A. Jasperson,Vivek Oommen,David L. Damm,Krishna Garikipati,Remi Dingreville*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 潜在神经代理求解器在长时间自回归推演中误差快速累积，根源不在潜在表示本身，而在于仅以重构目标训练。通过Koopman算子学习、Hamming噪声注入与多步推演微调等训练层面干预，可将长程误差降低约40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在动力学模型通过演化压缩的潜在空间来加速时变物理系统模拟，理论上可降低计算成本并简化学习。但实际中误差在长时间自回归推演中迅速累积，严重限制其预测价值。作者希望厘清这一不稳定性的真正来源，并找到改善长时程预测的训练策略。

Method: 作者论证不稳定性并非源于潜在表示本身，而是源于仅以重构为目标训练所产生的表示不适合长期预测。为此系统评估了一系列训练层面的干预措施：在自编码器训练中引入Koopman算子学习与Hamming噪声注入以改善压缩质量；在动力学建模中引入噪声注入与多步推演微调。并考察这些干预对重构、单步预测等常规指标的影响。

Result: 改善长时程推演稳定性的干预往往会使重构和单步预测等常规训练指标变差。但整体而言，这些干预将长程推演误差降低约40%，在两个物理基准上达到或超过全分辨率模型的精度，同时浮点运算量减少两个数量级、GPU内存减半。应用于高周疲劳的介观晶体塑性模拟时，所得代理模型可在远超训练所见的时程上稳定外推。

Conclusion: 神经压缩不应仅以降维为目的，而应重构解空间以支持稳定的动力学演化。这是科学应用中可靠、高效神经代理模型的关键要求。

Abstract: Latent neural surrogate solvers, or latent dynamics models, accelerate simulations of time-dependent physical systems by evolving a compressed latent space rather than resolving full-resolution fields directly. In principle this reduces computational cost and simplifies learning, but in practice errors often accumulate rapidly during long autoregressive rollouts, limiting predictive utility. We show that this instability does not stem from the latent representation itself, but arises when it is trained solely for reconstruction, producing representations poorly suited to long-horizon forecasting. We systematically evaluate training-level interventions that align latent representations with long-horizon rollout: Koopman operator learning and Hamming noise injection during autoencoder training to improve compression, together with noise injection and multi-step rollout fine-tuning to improve dynamics. Interventions that improve long-horizon rollout stability often degrade conventional training metrics, including reconstruction and one-step prediction accuracy. Collectively, these interventions reduce long-rollout error by approximately 40\% and match or exceed the accuracy of full-resolution models on two physics benchmarks, while requiring 2 orders of magnitude fewer floating point operations and half the GPU memory. Applied to mesoscale crystal-plasticity simulations of high-cycle fatigue, the resulting surrogate achieves stable extrapolation over horizons orders of magnitude beyond those observed during training. More broadly, these results show that neural compression should be designed not merely to reduce dimensionality, but to restructure the solution space for stable dynamical evolution, a key requirement for reliable, efficient neural surrogates in scientific applications.

</details>
